Architecture plan:
- A single Cloudflare Worker with static assets (Workers with assets binding — the modern way, replacing Pages Functions). Frontend: plain HTML/CSS/JS (no build step, keeps things simple). Backend: Worker API routes + D1 (users, comics, chapters, danmaku, orders) + R2 (comic page images).
- Auth: session cookies with httpOnly, password hashing via WebCrypto (PBKDF2).
- Upload: authors upload images to R2 via the Worker (multipart form).
- Reader: page/scroll mode, danmaku overlay tied to page index and time.
- Payment: mock — order creation, "simulated payment success" endpoint.
- Watermark: canvas overlay with username, disable right-click.

Use JS (not TS) to keep the deploy pipeline simple? Wrangler handles TS natively via esbuild. TS is nice but not required. Let's use plain JS modules for simplicity, or lightly-typed TS. Going with JS to avoid type gymnastics.

Key design:
- Worker with assets binding (`assets` + `main`), routes: /api/*
- D1 schema:
  - users(id, username, pass_hash, pass_salt, role, created_at)
  - comics(id, owner_id, title, description, cover, status, created_at)
  - chapters(id, comic_id, num, title, created_at)
  - pages(id, chapter_id, idx, r2_key, width?, height?)
  - danmaku(id, chapter_id, page_idx, t_ms, text, user_id, created_at)
  - orders(id, user_id, comic_id, amount_cents, status, created_at, paid_at) — mock payment
  - Probably a membership/unlock table: unlocks(user_id, comic_id)
- Auth: PBKDF2 via WebCrypto for password hashing, sessions stored in KV or as a signed cookie (HMAC). Simplest: sessions table in D1 with token, httpOnly cookie.
- Upload: POST /api/upload with multipart, images stored in R2. Serve images at /api/img/{key} — for watermarking paid content, only serve authenticated owners/unlockers; for logged-in users, dynamically overlay watermark text on the client side.
- Client-side watermark: div repeated with diagonal text (username + id). Disable contextmenu on the reader, hide images in copy. Be honest that this is deterrent-only.
- Danmaku: fetch the danmaku list per chapter, overlay animated spans moving horizontally across the top of the current page based on page index + timer; input to POST new danmaku.
- Mock payment: comic can be marked paid (price_cents). "Unlock" button creates an order, then a "mock pay" endpoint flips status to paid and inserts an unlock.
- Editing: owner can edit comic metadata, add/delete chapters and pages.

With not_found_handling's single-page-app, every unknown path returns index.html. But I want to use multipage (index.html, login.html, reader.html, etc.). In fact, with the "single-page-app" fallback, /reader would return index.html, so it wouldn't work for multipage. Better to use the default "404-page"? With the default behavior, assets serve /login.html at the /login.html path, and for clean URLs, the router could handle, say, /reader?comics=1, keeping the HTML file as-is and using query params. Simplest is default not_found_handling (fall back to 404.html or index?) — the Workers static asset default behavior: exact path match, and implicit /foo.html -> /foo. Actually, with the assets binding, does the root directory serve /reader as reader.html? The default routing for Workers with assets: if no exact match, then /path/index.html, then /path.html (with "html_handling" enabled by default — "AutoDirIndex" and ".html" extension fallback enabled). Right, default html_handling auto-converts /reader to reader.html. So I just need to remove not_found_handling, or set it to default. Let me fix wrangler.jsonc.

Schema:
```sql
CREATE TABLE users (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
  pass_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
  pass_salt TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE TABLE sessions (
  token TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE comics (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  owner_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  title TEXT NOT NULL,
  description TEXT DEFAULT '',
  cover_key TEXT,
  price_cents INTEGER DEFAULT 0,   -- 0 = free; otherwise unlock cost per comic
  published INTEGER DEFAULT 1,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE TABLE chapters (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  comic_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  num INTEGER NOT NULL,
  title TEXT DEFAULT '',
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE TABLE pages (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  chapter_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  idx INTEGER NOT NULL,
  r2_key TEXT NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE danmaku (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  chapter_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  page_idx INTEGER NOT NULL,
  t_ms INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  user_id INTEGER,
  text TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE TABLE orders (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  comic_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  amount_cents INTEGER NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending', -- pending|paid|cancelled
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  paid_at TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE unlocks (
  user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  comic_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  PRIMARY KEY (user_id, comic_id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_chapters_comic ON chapters(comic_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_pages_chapter ON pages(chapter_id, idx);
CREATE INDEX idx_danmaku_chapter ON danmaku(chapter_id, page_idx);
```

After that, worker.js — the big one. Let's write it carefully but compactly.

Endpoints:
- POST /api/register {username,password} -> creates user + session cookie
- POST /api/login {username,password}
- POST /api/logout
- GET /api/me
- GET /api/comics -> list of published ones (with owner name, chapter count)
- GET /api/comics/:id -> details + chapters (with unlocked/paid flags)
- POST /api/comics (auth) {title, description, price_cents} -> create own comic
- PUT /api/comics/:id (auth owner) edit
- DELETE /api/comics/:id (auth owner) — delete chapters/pages/R2 objects
- POST /api/comics/:id/chapters (auth owner) {title, num?} auto num
- DELETE /api/chapters/:id
- POST /api/chapters/:id/pages (multipart, auth owner) files -> save to R2, insert pages
- DELETE /api/pages/:id
- GET /api/chapters/:id/reader -> pages + unlock check (first N pages free? Simply: if paid, either all free or all locked; but preview is nice: if paid, lock everything after the first 3 pages). To make payment demoable, keep "if price>0 and not unlocked, lock pages after the first 3."
- GET /api/img/:key -> authenticated delivery: query param ch (chapter id); check unlock/ownership/free; return R2 object with cache private.
- GET /api/danmaku?chapter=&page= -> list
- POST /api/danmaku {chapterId, pageIdx, tMs, text} (auth)
- POST /api/orders {comicId} (auth) -> pending order
- POST /api/orders/:id/pay (auth) -> mock payment: mark as paid + unlock

Password hashing: PBKDF2 with WebCrypto, 100k iterations, SHA-256, random salt, store as hex.

Session: random 32-byte token hex, cookie `sid`, HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Path=/, Max-Age 30 days; row in sessions table with expiry.

Multipart upload: use request.formData(), files as File[]. Size limit via MAX_UPLOAD_MB. Only allow image content types.

Image delivery key path: `users/{uid}/comics/{cid}/chapters/{chid}/{uuid}.jpg` or similar. r2_key stored in pages; img route: GET /api/img/{key} — key contains slashes; if run_worker_first [/api/*] is in place, the Worker receives the path and can slice.

Watermarking: after unlock/auth, the client overlays the username. The image itself is served without watermark (honestly).

Content safety: escape danmaku text on the client side (textContent). SQL: use prepared statements via db.prepare with bind everywhere.

Rate limiting: skip (prototype).

Frontend: 4 HTML files + shared CSS + JS.
- index.html: header (login state), comic grid.
- login.html: toggle between login/register form.
- dashboard.html: create/edit own comics, upload chapters/pages.
- reader.html?ch=ID: page display, danmaku overlay + input, watermark, contextmenu disabled, prev/next, progress bar, scroll mode and page mode.

Keep styles minimal dark theme. Danmaku: CSS animation moving right to left; spawner processes danmaku in time order for the current page (t_ms relative to page display time).

Now let's write the files. First fix wrangler.jsonc (remove single-page-app), then migration, then worker.js, then frontend.

A small fix: if you use `single-page-app` for the assets fallback, the multi-page site ends up broken, so use the default multi-page routing instead.

Let's write a complete Worker API in a single file, well organized. Plain JS.

Main helper functions:
- json(data, status, headers)
- hashPassword(password, saltHex) -> PBKDF2 150000 iters sha256 hex
- randomHex(n)
- getSession(request, env) -> parse cookie sid, query sessions join users
- requireAuth

Routing via URL pattern matching. Path like /api/img/<key...>.

Free preview logic: PREVIEW_PAGES = 3. If comic.price_cents>0 && not unlocked && not owner → lock pages beyond the first 3 (per chapter? Across the whole comic? Per chapter feels natural: first 3 pages of each paid chapter are free). Simpler rule: if comic is paid and not unlocked, only the first 3 pages of each chapter are viewable.

Reader API response: {comic, chapter, pages:[{idx,url,locked}], unlocked:bool} — locked pages have url null.

Danmaku GET: ?chapterId&pageIdx -> rows. POST: auth required, text up to 100 chars, chapter must exist.

Orders: POST /api/orders {comicId} → if already unlocked or free → 400; create a pending order and return orderId. POST /api/orders/:id/pay → mock: set paid, insert unlock.

Comic list: GET /api/comics?mine=1 → mine (auth). Public list: published=1, join owner's username, chapter count. Cover: if no cover_key, use first page of first chapter? Simpler: cover from a cover upload, and if absent the client shows a placeholder. Or on the server side, compute the cover as the first page of the latest chapter: subquery in SQL. For a clean grid, let's compute cover_key fallback via join — do it with per-comic subquery:
```sql
SELECT c.*, u.username AS owner, 
 (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chapters ch WHERE ch.comic_id=c.id) AS chapter_count,
 COALESCE(c.cover_key, (SELECT p.r2_key FROM pages p JOIN chapters ch ON ch.id=p.chapter_id WHERE ch.comic_id=c.id ORDER BY ch.num, p.idx LIMIT 1)) AS cover
FROM comics c JOIN users u ON u.id=c.owner_id WHERE c.published=1 ORDER BY c.created_at DESC
```
Cover images: the /api/img route requires chapter context for auth… covers need to be public. Hmm — the auth model for images: /api/img/* checks either (a) the key belongs to a comic that is free or the user has unlocked/owns it, or (b) it's the comic's cover/first-page preview. Simpler: /api/img checks the page row by r2_key:
- Page not found (e.g. cover) → if the comic is free or the requester is the owner, allow, otherwise deny? Covers should be viewable by everyone (since it's marketing). Let's do this: covers are allowed via a ?cover=1 query param → look up the comic by cover_key and serve. For pages: ?ch=chapterId to check the lock; further limit: if paid & not unlocked, only idx < 3 is allowed.
Actually, an even simpler unified proposal: GET /api/img/{key}?ctx=... let's implement the checks:
1. Search pages by r2_key → join chapter → join comic: owner or free or unlocked → serve; otherwise if page.idx < PREVIEW_PAGES → serve; else 403.
2. Search comics by cover_key → anyone can serve (covers are for promotion).
3. Otherwise 404.
This way we don't even need a ch param.

Upload endpoints:
- POST /api/comics : JSON {title, description, price_cents, published} (multipart with optional cover? Keep cover as a separate POST /api/comics/:id/cover with a single image as multipart "file").
- POST /api/comics/:id/chapters : JSON {title} → num = max+1.
- POST /api/chapters/:id/pages : multipart "files" multiple images → r2 key `c{comicId}/ch{chapterId}/{ts}-{i}-{rand}.{ext}`; owner check; pages idx continues from existing max.
- DELETE /api/chapters/:id (owner) delete pages + R2 + danmaku + chapter.
- DELETE /api/pages/:id (owner) + reindex? Keep the holes in idx (ordering by idx still works). OK.
- PUT /api/comics/:id (owner) update fields. Cover set via the cover endpoint.
- DELETE /api/comics/:id (owner): delete all chapters/pages/R2/danmaku/orders? cascade delete via a query.

Image validation: contentType is one of {image/jpeg,png,webp,gif,avif}. Size cap env.MAX_UPLOAD_MB, per-file cap and total (multipart body overall ≤ Worker limits — is body 25MB on paid? Free Workers have a 100MB body limit? Actually Worker request body limit is 100MB). Number of files per request: cap at 50.

R2 put with httpMetadata contentType.

Serving: fetch from bucket, return with Cache-Control private no-store (to prevent CDN caching of paid images), X-Content-Type-Options nosniff, and Referrer-Policy.

Also the reader needs the chapter list for the current comic: GET /api/comics/:id returns chapters with page counts.

Registration username validation: 2–30 chars [\w一-鿿-]+, allow CJK. Password ≥6 chars.

Cookie: `sid`. Also set on the register/login response.

Logout: delete session row + expire cookie.

GET /api/me → {id, username} or null.

{
  "content": "/**\n * 漫画平台 Worker API\n * 路由前缀 /api/*，其余路径由静态资源(assets)处理。\n */\n\nconst PREVIEW_PAGES = 3; // 付费漫画每章可免费试读的页数\nconst SESSION_DAYS = 30;\nconst MAX_DANMAKU_LEN = 100;\nconst ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = new Set([\n  \"image/jpeg\", \"image/png\", \"image/webp\", \"image/gif\", \"image/avif\",\n]);\n\n// ---------- 小工具 ----------\n\nfunction json(data, status = 200, headers = {}) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\n    status,\n    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\", ...headers },\n  });\n}\n\nconst err = (msg, status = 400) => json({ error: msg }, status);\n\nfunction randomHex(bytes) {\n  const a = new Uint8Array(bytes);\n  crypto.getRandomValues(a);\n  return [...a].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n}\n\nasync function hashPassword(password, saltHex) {\n  const enc = new TextEncoder();\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\"raw\", enc.encode(password), \"PBKDF2\", false, [\"deriveBits\"]);\n  const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);\n  const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(\n    { name: \"PBKDF2\", salt, iterations: 150_000, hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n    key, 256\n  );\n  return bufToHex(bits);\n}\n\nconst hexToBuf = (h) => Uint8Array.from(h.match(/../g).map((x) => parseInt(x, 16)));\nconst bufToHex = (b) => [...new Uint8Array(b)].map((x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n\nfunction sessionCookie(token, maxAgeDays = SESSION_DAYS) {\n  const maxAge = maxAgeDays > 0 ? maxAgeDays * 86400 : 0;\n  return `sid=${token}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=${maxAge}`;\n}\n\nasync function parseBody(request) {\n  try { return await request.json(); } catch { return null; }\n}\n\n// ---------- 会话 / 鉴权 ----------\n\nasync function currentUser(request, env) {\n  const cookie = request.headers.get(\"cookie\") || \"\";\n  const m = cookie.match(/(?:^|;\\s*)sid=([0-9a-f]{64})/);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  const row = await env.DB.prepare(\n    `SELECT u.id, u.username, s.token, s.expires_at FROM sessions s\n     JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id WHERE s.token = ?`\n  ).bind(m[1]).first();\n  if (!row) return null;\n  if (row.expires_at < Date.now()) {\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token = ?\").bind(m[1]).run();\n    return null;\n  }\n  return row;\n}\n\nasync function createSession(env, userId) {\n  const token = randomHex(32);\n  await env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT INTO sessions (token, user_id, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n    .bind(token, userId, Date.now() + SESSION_DAYS * 86400_000).run();\n  return token;\n}\n\n// ---------- 权限检查 ----------\n\nasync function getComicWithAccess(env, comicId, user) {\n  const comic = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId).first();\n  if (!comic) return null;\n  let unlocked = comic.price_cents <= 0;\n  if (!unlocked && user && user.id === comic.owner_id) unlocked = true;\n  if (!unlocked && user) {\n    const u = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT 1 FROM unlocks WHERE user_id = ? AND comic_id = ?\")\n      .bind(user.id, comicId).first();\n    unlocked = !!u;\n  }\n  const isOwner = !!(user && user.id === comic.owner_id);\n  return { comic, unlocked, isOwner };\n}\n\nasync function requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, comicId) {\n  if (!user) return { e: err(\"需要登录\", 401) };\n  const comic = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId).first();\n  if (!comic) return { e: err(\"漫画不存在\", 404) };\n  if (comic.owner_id !== user.id) return { e: err(\"只有作者可以操作自己的漫画\", 403) };\n  return { comic };\n}\n\nasync function requireOwnerOfChapter(env, user, chapterId) {\n  if (!user) return { e: err(\"需要登录\", 401) };\n  const ch = await env.DB.prepare(\n    \"SELECT ch.*, c.owner_id FROM chapters ch JOIN comics c ON c.id = ch.comic_id WHERE ch.id = ?\"\n  ).bind(chapterId).first();\n  if (!ch) return { e: err(\"章节不存在\", 404) };\n  if (ch.owner_id !== user.id) return { e: err(\"只有作者可以操作\", 403) };\n  return { chapter: ch };\n}\n\n// ---------- 上传处理 ----------\n\nasync function readImages(form, env, field = \"files\") {\n  const entries = form.getAll(field).filter((f) => f instanceof File && f.size > 0);\n  if (entries.length === 0) return { e: err(\"没有收到文件\") };\n  if (entries.length > 100) return { e: err(\"单次最多上传 100 张\") };\n  const maxBytes = Number(env.MAX_UPLOAD_MB || 10) * 1024 * 1024;\n  for (const f of entries) {\n    if (!ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.has(f.type)) return { e: err(`不支持的文件类型: ${f.type || f.name}`) };\n    if (f.size > maxBytes) return { e: err(`文件 ${f.name} 超过 ${env.MAX_UPLOAD_MB || 10}MB 限制`) };\n  }\n  return { files: entries };\n}\n\nasync function putImage(env, key, file) {\n  await env.BUCKET.put(key, file.stream(), {\n    httpMetadata: { contentType: file.type, cacheControl: \"private, no-store\" },\n  });\n  return key;\n}\n\n// ---------- API 路由 ----------\n\nconst routes = [\n  // ===== 账号 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/register$/, async (c, env) => {\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req);\n    if (!body) return err(\"需要 JSON body\");\n    const username = String(body.username || \"\").trim();\n    const password = String(body.password || \"\");\n    if (!/^[\\w\\p{Script=Han}.\\-]{2,30}$/u.test(username)) return err(\"用户名需 2-30 个字符（文字/数字/_-.）\");\n    if (password.length < 6) return err(\"密码至少 6 位\");\n    const exists = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE username = ?\").bind(username).first();\n    if (exists) return err(\"用户名已被占用\", 409);\n    const salt = randomHex(16);\n    const hash = await hashPassword(password, salt);\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT INTO users (username, pass_hash, pass_salt) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(username, hash, salt).run();\n    const token = await createSession(env, meta.last_row_id);\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id, username }, 201, { \"set-cookie\": sessionCookie(token) });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/login$/, .bind(String(body.username || \"\").trim()).first();\n    if (!user) return err(\"用户名或密码错误\", 401);\n    const hash = await hashPassword(String(body.password || \"\"), user.pass_salt);\n    if (hash !== user.pass_hash) return err(\"用户名或密码错误\", 401);\n    const token = await createSession(env, user.id);\n    return json({ id: user.id, username: user.username }, 200, { \"set-cookie\": sessionCookie(token) });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/logout$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (user) await env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token = ?\").bind(user.token).run();\n { id: null })],\n\n  // ===== 漫画浏览 =====\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/comics$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    const mine = new URL(c.req.url).searchParams.get(\"mine\");\n    const sql = `\n      SELECT c.*, u.username AS owner_name,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chapters ch WHERE ch.comic_id = c.id) AS chapter_count,\n        COALESCE(c.cover_key,\n          (SELECT p.r2_key FROM pages p JOIN chapters ch ON ch.id = p.chapter_id\n           WHERE ch.comic_id = c.id ORDER BY ch.num, p.idx LIMIT 1)) AS cover\n      FROM comics c JOIN users u ON u.id = c.owner_id\n      ${mine && user ? \"WHERE c.owner_id = ?\" : \"WHERE c.published = 1\"}\n      ORDER BY c.created_at DESC, c.id DESC`;\n    const stmt = env.DB.prepare(sql);\n    const { results } = await (mine && user ? stmt.bind(user.id) : stmt).all();\n    return json({ comics: results });\n  }],\n\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const acc = await getComicWithAccess(env, Number(m[1]), user);\n    if (!acc) return err(\"漫画不存在\", 404);\n    if (!acc.comic.published && !acc.isOwner) return err(\"漫画不存在\", 404);\n    const { results: chapters } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"SELECT id, num, title, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pages p WHERE p.chapter_id = ch.id) AS page_count FROM chapters ch WHERE comic_id = ? ORDER BY num\")\n      .bind(acc.comic.id).all();\n    return json({\n      comic: acc.comic, owner: null, chapters,\n      unlocked: acc.unlocked, isOwner: acc.isOwner, previewPages: PREVIEW_PAGES,\n    });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 作者：漫画 CRUD =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/comics$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const title = String(body.title || \"\").trim();\n    if (!title || title.length > 80) return err(\"标题需 1-80 字符\");\n    const price = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(body.price_cents) || 0));\n    const published = body.published === false ? 0 : 1;\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO comics (owner_id, title, description, price_cents, published) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(user.id, title, String(body.description || \"\").slice(0, 2000), price, published).run();\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"PUT\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const title = body.title !== undefined ? String(body.title).trim() : r.comic.title;\n    if (!title || title.length > 80) return err(\"标题需 1-80 字符\");\n    const desc = body.description !== undefined ? String(body.description).slice(0, 2000) : r.comic.description;\n    const price = body.price_cents !== undefined ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(body.price_cents) || 0)) : r.comic.price_cents;\n    const published = body.published !== undefined ? (body.published ? 1 : 0) : r.comic.published;\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE comics SET title=?, description=?, price_cents=?, published=? WHERE id=?\")\n      .bind(title, desc, price, published, r.comic.id).run();\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  [\"DELETE\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    await deleteComicCascade(env, r.comic.id);\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)\\/cover$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    const form = await c.req.formData();\n    const files = form.getAll(\"file\").filter((f) => f instanceof File && f.size > 0);\n    if (files.length !== 1) return err(\"需要 1 个封面文件\");\n    const one = await readImages({ getAll: () => files }, env);\n    if (one.e) return one.e;\n    if (r.comic.cover_key) await env.BUCKET.delete(r.comic.cover_key);\n    const key = `c${r.comic.id}/cover/${Date.now()}-${randomHex(4)}`;\n    await putImage(env, key, one.files[0]);\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE comics SET cover_key=? WHERE id=?\").bind(key, r.comic.id).run();\n    return json({ cover_key: key });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 作者：章节 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)\\/chapters$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO chapters (comic_id, num, title) VALUES (?, (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(num),0)+1 FROM chapters WHERE comic_id = ?), ?)\")\n      .bind(r.comic.id, r.comic.id, String(body.title || \"\").slice(0, 80)).run();\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"DELETE\", /^\\/api\\/chapters\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfChapter(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    await deleteChapterCascade(env, r.chapter.id);\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 作者：页面上传 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/chapters\\/(\\d+)\\/pages$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfChapter(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    let form;\n    try { form = await c.req.formData(); } catch { return err(\"multipart 解析失败\"); }\n    const files = await readImages(form, env);\n    if (files.e) return files.e;\n    const max = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(idx), -1) AS mx FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ?\")\n      .bind(r.chapter.id).first();\n    let idx = max.mx + 1;\n    const added = [];\n    for (const f of files.files) {\n      const ext = (f.name.split(\".\").pop() || \"img\").toLowerCase().slice(0, 8);\n      const key = `c${r.chapter.comic_id}/ch${r.chapter.id}/p${idx}-${Date.now()}-${randomHex(4)}.${ext}`;\n      await putImage(env, key, f);\n      await env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT INTO pages (chapter_id, idx, r2_key) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n        .bind(r.chapter.id, idx, key).run();\n      added.push({ idx, r2_key: key });\n      idx++;\n    }\n    return json({ added: added.length, pages: added }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"DELETE\", /^\\/api\\/pages\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const page = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"SELECT p.*, c.owner_id FROM pages p JOIN chapters ch ON ch.id = p.chapter_id JOIN comics c ON c.id = ch.comic_id WHERE p.id = ?\")\n      .bind(Number(m[1])).first();\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    if (!page) return err(\"页面不存在\", 404);\n    if (page.owner_id !== user.id) return err(\"只有作者可以操作\", 403);\n    await env.BUCKET.delete(page.r2_key);\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\").bind(page.id).run();\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 阅读器 =====\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/chapters\\/(\\d+)\\/reader$/, async (c, ORDER BY idx\").bind(ch.id).all();\n    const canSee = (p) => acc.unlocked || acc.isOwner || p.idx < PREVIEW_PAGES;\n    return json({\n      chapter: { id: ch.id, num: ch.num, title: ch.title, comic_id: ch.comic_id, comic_title: ch.comic_title },\n      unlocked: acc.unlocked, isOwner: acc.isOwner, price_cents: ch.price_cents,\n      pages: pages.map((p) => ({\n        id: p.id, idx: p.idx,\n        url: canSee(p) ? AND d.page_idx = ? ORDER BY d.t_ms LIMIT 500`)\n      .bind(chId, pageIdx).all();\n    return json({ danmaku: results });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/danmaku$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录后发送弹幕\", 401);\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const text = String(body.text || \"\").trim().slice(0, MAX_DANMAKU_LEN);\n    if (!text) return err(\"弹幕内容不能为空\");\n    const chId = Number(body.chapterId);\n    const pageIdx = Number(body.pageIdx);\n    const tMs = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(body.tMs) || 0));\n    const ch = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT 1 FROM chapters WHERE id = ?\").bind(chId).first();\n    if (!ch) return err(\"章节不存在\", 404);\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO danmaku (chapter_id, page_idx, t_ms, user_id, text) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(chId, pageIdx, tMs, user.id, text).run();\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id, text }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  // ===== Mock 支付 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/orders$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const acc = await getComicWithAccess(env, Number(body.comicId), user);\n    if (!acc) return err(\"漫画不存在\", 404);\n    if (acc.comic.price_cents <= 0) return err(\"该漫画免费\");\n    if (acc.unlocked) return err(\"已购买\");\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO orders (user_id, comic_id, amount_cents) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(user.id, acc.comic.id, acc.comic.price_cents).run();\n    return json({ orderId: meta.last_row_id, amount_cents: acc.comic.price_cents, comicTitle: acc.comic.title }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/orders\\/(\\d+)\\/pay$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const order = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM orders WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?\")\n      .bind(Number(m[1]), user.id).first();\n    if (!order) return err(\"订单不存在\", 404);\n    if (order.status === \"paid\") return json({ ok: true, alreadyPaid: true });\n    // Mock：直接标记支付成功并解锁（生产环境应替换为支付宝异步通知验签后解锁）\n    await env.DB.batch([\n      env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE orders SET status='paid', paid_at=datetime('now') WHERE id=?\").bind(order.id),\n      env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO unlocks (user_id, comic_id) VALUES (?, ?)\").bind(user.id, order.comic_id),\n    ]);\n    return json({ ok: true, unlocked: true });\n  }],\n\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/orders$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      `SELECT o.id, o.amount_cents, o.status, o.created_at, c.title AS comic_title\n       FROM orders o JOIN comics c ON c.id = o.comic_id WHERE o.user_id = ? ORDER BY o.id DESC LIMIT 100`)\n      .bind(user.id).all();\n    return json({ orders: results });\n  }],\n];\n\n// ---------- 级联删除 ----------\n\nasync function deleteChapterCascade(env, chapterId) {\n  const { results: pages } = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT r2_key FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ?\").bind(chapterId).all();\n  for (const p of pages) await env.BUCKET.delete(p.r2_key);\n  await env.DB.batch([\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ?\").bind(chapterId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM danmaku WHERE chapter_id = ?\").bind(chapterId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM chapters WHERE id = ?\").bind(chapterId),\n  ]);\n}\n\nasync function deleteComicCascade(env, comicId) {\n  const { results: chs } = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT id FROM chapters WHERE comic_id = ?\").bind(comicId).all();\n  for (const ch of chs) await deleteChapterCascade(env, ch.id);\n  const comic = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT cover_key FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId).first();\n  if (comic?.cover_key) await env.BUCKET.delete(comic.cover_key);\n  await env.DB.batch([\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM orders WHERE comic_id = ?\").bind(comicId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM unlocks WHERE comic_id = ?\").bind(comicId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// ---------- fetch 入口 ----------\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const path = url.pathname;\n    if (!path.startsWith(\"/api/\")) {\n      // 交给静态资源（正常情况下 assets 层已处理，这里兜底 404）\n      return env.ASSETS.fetch(request);\n    }\n    const user = await currentUser(request, env);\n    for (const [method, pattern, handler] of routes) {\n      if (request.method !== method) continue;\n      const m = path.match(pattern);\n      if (!m) continue;\n      try {\n        return await handler(request, env, user, m);\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error(`[api] ${method} ${path}`, e);\n        return err(\"服务器内部错误\", 500);\n      }\n    }\n    return err(\"接口不存在\", 404);\n  },\n};\n",

}

To avoid XSS from comic titles / danmaku (bullet-comments) / usernames, when constructing DOM with innerHTML templates, always escape with esc(). esc = s.replace(/[&<>"']/...). Danmaku rendering: use textContent.

Header HTML: logo → /, nav: 书城 (Book City) /, 我的创作 (My Creations) /dashboard, auth: 登录 (Login) /login or username dropdown (退出 (Logout) button).

Footer note about prototype & mock payments.

index.html: grid of comic cards. Card: cover img (`/api/img/${cover}` if cover exists, otherwise placeholder div), title, poster, chapter count, price (¥ x.xx or 免费 (Free)).

login.html: two tabs for 登录 (Login) / 注册 (Register); POST → redirect to `next` param or '/'.

comic.html?id=N: fetch /api/comics/:id → info, purchase button (if price>0 && !unlocked && !owner), chapter list → /reader?ch=.
Purchase modal: display amount, 模拟支付 (Simulate Payment) button → POST /api/orders → POST /api/orders/:id/pay → location.reload(). Small note "原型版模拟支付,未接真实支付宝 (Prototype version is simulated payment, real Alipay not connected)".

reader.html?ch=N: fetch /api/chapters/N/reader.
- Top bar: back, comic title, chapter number/title, mode toggle (翻页 (Page Turn) / 滚动 (Scroll)), danmaku on/off.
- Page mode: display img in container .stage; click left/right halves or arrow keys to switch; page counter.
- Scroll mode: column of all loaded img.
- Danmaku system per current page index: list from GET /api/danmaku?chapterId&pageIdx; each item has t_ms; page timer starts at display time; spawner via requestAnimationFrame or setInterval every 100ms moves due items (t_ms <= elapsed, unspawned) from right to left across the .dm-layer. Position in top lane (random 0..laneMax * 36px). Remove span after animation ends (8s). Send: input + button → POST with tMs=elapsed, render own comment immediately. Poll every 5 seconds for new items with t_ms <= elapsed? New comments have arbitrary t_ms; poll every 5 seconds, spawn items where t_ms is within [elapsed-100, elapsed+... hmm, more simply: spawn when t_ms <= elapsed && !spawned; poll and include — delayed spawn but no problem.
- Lock: if page.url is null → display lock panel with 购买 (Purchase) button (open purchase flow via API order/pay inline), reload reader data after unlock.
- Watermark: overlay div, JS fills with rotated spans of `username · id=..` ×40, pointer-events:none, z-index on top of everything within stage.
- Guard: document contextmenu is preventDefault in reader; img dragstart is prevented; Ctrl+S and Ctrl+P keydown are preventDefault; on PrintScreen keydown → clear clipboard + toast "已检测截图尝试 (Screenshot attempt detected)" (simply); CSS user-select none.
- Honest note: toast text states "cannot stop system screenshots".

dashboard.html:

- When comic selected: detail (inline edit of title/desc/price/published, cover upload, delete comic), chapter list (add chapter button, delete chapter, pages per chapter: expand → list pages as thumbnails with delete, upload input multiple → POST formData).
- Also "阅读 (Read)" link to first chapter/reader.

#### Thinking

Next is reader.html — the most complex part: page/scroll modes, per-page danmaku engine, watermark, guards, lock panel + purchase.

Danmaku engine design:
- state: pages[], curIdx, pageStart (performance.now()), spawned Set, items[] from GET (each element {id, t_ms, text, username}).
- tick every 200ms: elapsed = now - pageStart; spawn items where t_ms <= elapsed and not yet spawned → create span in lane.
- spawn(text): span.dm, top = lane*34 random, duration 7 + rand*3 seconds; append to #dm-layer; remove on animationend.
- Poll every 5 seconds: re-fetch danmaku, add new ids to items (if t_ms <= elapsed, spawn on next tick).
- Send: POST /api/danmaku {chapterId, pageIdx: pages[cur].idx, tMs: elapsed}; locally spawn + push to items.
- On page change: reset pageStart, clear layer, spawned set retains the same items (danmaku is stored per page_idx).

Watermark: build 40 spans, text `username #id @MangaZone`, positioned at random-ish grid intervals, fixed rotation. Placed inside stage (absolute inset 0). Scroll mode: watermark only covers stage's visible area... stage wraps everything in scroll mode; no problem, set #watermark's inset to 0 to cover all content height.

Page mode: display current page's image; locked page → lock panel with purchase button. Prev/Next via arrows/click zones, page counter at top bar.

Scroll mode: stage holds all viewable images stacked vertically. Danmaku in scroll mode: danmaku tied to page timer is complex — simplify: in scroll mode, fetch all pages' danmaku? Disable danmaku spawn in scroll mode but keep send possible (attach to nearest visible page?). Simpler: in scroll mode, hide danmaku layer, disable input with tooltip. Acceptable for prototype: scroll mode = pure continuous reading without danmaku. But that's weird. Alternative: in scroll mode, compute current page by scroll position (the page whose top has been passed), and use per-page timer from time of first view. This is better but adds code. Go with midpoint detection: on scroll, determine the page containing viewport center; track its displayStart; spawn danmaku for that page; layer is fixed fullscreen (position:fixed inset:0) so it floats over the entire viewport. Position layer in fixed over viewport in both modes? In page mode, stage is roughly viewport-sized; fixed overlay covering viewport is visually fine. Simplification: make #dm-layer fixed to viewport. Watermark needs to follow content in scroll mode — make watermark fixed too? Watermark on top of viewport (fixed) with pointer-events none also works, stays displayed during scrolling. Both fixed = simpler and more robust.

But the .stage overflow hidden clip was for danmaku... fixed layer doesn't need clip, has its own overflow hidden.

So: dm-layer and watermark are position fixed inset 0, z-index 20/30, pointer-events none. Danmaku animation translateX(100vw) → no problem.

Current page detection in scroll mode: give each img a data-idx; on scroll (throttled with rAF), find the last img whose rect.top <= innerHeight/2.

Timer: on pageIdx change: pageStart[pageIdx] ||= performance.now(); elapsed is computed per current page.

Spawn loop: track spawnedLastId per page? Track with Set of item ids that have been spawned. Items only for current page (fetch per page; prefetch next page's danmaku on load? Simpler: on current page change → fetch its danmaku, reset tick for that list).

In page mode with arrow advance: same — fetch per page.

Locked pages in scroll mode: show lock panel inline in the list.

Purchase in reader: import purchaseDialog; after success, re-fetch reader data.

Keyboard: ArrowLeft/Right, A/D? Left/right arrows only + Space for next.

Guards:
- document.addEventListener('contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault())
- img dragstart prevent (set draggable false)
- keydown: Ctrl+S, Ctrl+P, Ctrl+Shift+I? preventDefault (devtools cannot be blocked).
- PrintScreen keydown: try clipboard item write to overwrite; toast "截屏行为已记录并上报水印ID（模拟）" [Screenshot action has been logged and watermark ID reported (simulated)] — honestly: display that it was detected; actual reporting is not implemented. Keep text: "检测到截屏按键 · 本页面水印含你的账户ID，可用于溯源" [Screenshot key detected · The watermark on this page contains your account ID, usable for traceability].
- CSS user-select none on stage area.

Now let's write reader.html.

The D1 remote migration was applied. ImageGen failed (not retriable — service may not be available). Alternative test images: use a pure-JS PNG encoder to generate simple placeholder PNGs in Node? Writing a minimal PNG encoder in Node: possible with zlib (Node has zlib deflate) — PNG format: signature + IHDR + IDAT (scanlines with filter 0, zlib deflated) + IEND. About 40 lines. Generate colored pages with text drawn on them? Text needs rendering — skip text, just a gradient solid-color image with borders. That's sufficient for demo purposes (numbered "page"). Could even encode the number visually with a simple pixel pattern... Keep it simple: gradient + large colored blocks; for the demo it's enough to verify the pipeline.

n < 256; n++) { c = n; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1; t[n] = c; }\n    return t;\n  })());\n  c = -1;\n  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) c = table[(c ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);\n  return (c ^ -1) >>> 0;\n}\n\nfunction chunk(type, data) {\n  const len = Buffer.alloc(4); len.writeUInt32BE(data.length);\n  const td = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type), data]);\n  const crc = Buffer.alloc(4); crc.writeUInt32BE(crc32(td));\n  return Buffer.concat([len, td, crc]);\n}\n\nfunction makePng(w, h, painter) {\n  const raw = Buffer.alloc(h * (1 + w * 3));\n  let o = 0;\n  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {\n    raw[o++] = 0;\n    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n      const [r, g, b] = painter(x, y);\n      raw[o++] = r; raw[o++] = g; raw[o++] = b;\n    }\n  }\n  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);\n  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(w, 0); ihdr.writeUInt32BE(h, 4);\n  ihdr[8] = 8; ihdr[9] = 2; // 8-bit RGB\n  return Buffer.concat([\n    Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x09]),\n    chunk(\"IHDR\", ihdr),\n    chunk(\"IDAT\", zlib.deflateSync(raw, { level: 6 })),\n    chunk(\"IEND\", Buffer.alloc(0)),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// 用 5x7 位图点阵把页码画成大色块图案（辨识度足够测试用）\nconst DIGITS = {\n  \"0\": [\"11111\", \"10001\", \"10011\", \"10101\", \"11001\", \"10001\", \"11111\"],\n  \"1\": [\"00100\", \"01100\", \"00100\", \"00100\", \"00100\", \"00100\", \"01110\"],\n  \"2\": [\"11111\", \"00001\", \"11111\", \"10000\", \"10000\", \"10000\", \"11111\"],\n  \"3\": [\"11111\", \"00001\", \"11111\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"11111\"],\n  \"4\": [\"10001\", \"10001\", \"11111\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"00001\"],\n  \"5\": [\"11111\", \"10000\", \"11111\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"10001\", \"11111\"],\n};\n\nfunction painterFor(num, hue) {\n  const s = String(num);\n  const cell = 60, ox = 40, oy = 100;\n  const bg1 = hsl(hue, 30, 18), bg2 = hsl(hue + 40, 45, 38);\n  const fg = hsl(hue + 20, 85, 65);\n  return (x, y) => {\n    const t = y / 720;\n    const base = [\n      Math.round(bg1[0] + (bg2[0] - bg1[0]) * t),\n      Math.round(bg1[1] + (bg2[1] - bg1[1]) * t),\n      Math.round(bg1[2] + (bg2[2] - bg1[2]) * t),\n    ];\n    const di = Math.floor((x - ox) / cell);\n    const px = (x - ox) % cell, py = y - oy;\n    if (di >= 0 && di < s.length && py >= 0 && py < 7 * cell) {\n      const row = DIGITS[s[di]][Math.floor(py / cell)];\n      if (row[Math.floor(px / (cell / 5)) - 0] === undefined) return base;\n      if (row[Math.min(4, Math.floor(px / (cell / 5)))] === \"1\") return fg;\n    }\n    if (y > 640 && (Math.floor(x / 40) + Math.floor(y / 40)) % 2 === 0) return hsl(hue, 20, 26);\n    return base;\n  };\n}\n\nfunction hsl(h, s, l) {\n  s /= 100; l /= 100;\n  const k = (n) => (n + h / 30) % 12;\n  const a = s * Math.min(l, 1 - l);\n  const f = (n) => 255 * (l - a * Math.max(-1, Math.min(k(n) - 3, Math.min(9 - k(n), 1))));\n  return [Math.round(f(0)), Math.round(f(8)), Math.round(f(4))];\n}\n\nconst out = path.join(__dirname, \"..\", \"sample-assets\");\nfs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });\nfor (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {\n  const png = makePng(600, 720, painterFor(i, i * 47));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, `page${i}.png`), png);\n  console.log(`page${i}.png ${(png.length / 1024).toFixed(1)}KB`);\n}\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\user\\Documents\\Qoder\\2026-10-31\\59dfa34d\\scripts\\make-sample-pngs.js"
}
```

#### Thinking

Bug: The handler's signature is (c, env, user, m), but inside fetch it's called as handler(request, env, user, m). At line 176 of the GET /api/comics handler, `new URL(c.req.url)` is used — since the first argument passed is `request`, c.req is undefined... no wait, `request.req` doesn't exist. Right — the first argument of the handler is request, yet we're accessing c.req.url. Fix: call handler as a context object like { req: request }? The handler uses `c.req` (and formData also goes through c.req.formData()). Easiest is: pass `const c = { req: request }`. In fetch: `return await handler({ req: request }, env, user, m)`. Also, the read of `env.MAX_UPLOAD_MB` is fine.

Also currentUser(request, env) — OK.

Edit the handler call in worker.js.

Small bug: the handler argument is written as `c.req`, but the entry point is passing the raw `request`. Fix it to wrap it as a context object before passing it in.

#### Thinking

The readImages call in the cover endpoint is also hacky: `readImages({ getAll: () => files }, env)` — works because readImages calls form.getAll(field). No problem.

`ctx` is unused in fetch — no problem.

`env.ASSETS.fetch(request)` fallback — since run_worker_first is only for /api/*, non-/api requests won't reach the worker except for /api 404s... the worker only receives /api/*; if the API doesn't match, it returns err 404 instead of assets. The path where env.ASSETS would be called (non-/api) shouldn't occur; harmless.

```js
function chunk(type, data) {
  const len = Buffer.alloc(4); len.writeUInt32BE(data.length);
  const td = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type), data]);
  const crc = Buffer.alloc(4); crc.writeUInt32BE(crc32(td));
  return Buffer.concat([len, td, crc]);
}
```
This is correct.

crc32:
```js
function crc32(buf) {
  let c, table = crc32.t || (crc32.t = (() => {
    const t = new Int32Array(256);

    return t;
  })());
  c = -1;
  ...
}
```

Let me try a data URL test with base64 — earlier I built /tmp/eval.json but never ran it! Let me actually run it via mcp_call evaluate_script. The file is small (base64 is about 5.9KB).

Actually, a simpler hypothesis: Chrome is rejecting it because of *multiple IDATs*? No, there's only one.

Hmm, wait, let me reconsider: the base64 of a valid PNG starts with "iVBORw0KGgo". Mine started with "iVBORw0KGgk"! Let's see: bytes 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 09 00 → base64: iVBORw0KGgkA... The 10th byte 0x00 → "iVBORw0KGgo" is the standard for 8950 4E47 0D0A 1A09 0000 000D:
- 89 50 4E → iVBO
- 47 0D 0A → Rw0K
- 1A 09 00 → GgkA? Hmm: 1A=00011010, 09=00001001, 00=00000000 → 000110 010000 100100 000000 → 6,16,36,0 → iVBORw0KGgo uses "Gg"? Standard: bytes 1A 09 00 → indices 000110(6='G'? Base64 table: 6='G'? A-Z=0-25, so 6='G' yes), 010000(16='Q'), 100100(36='k'), 000000(0='A') → "GQkA"? Hmm, wait, base64 alphabet: A=0..Z=25, a=26..z=51, 0=52..9=61, +=62, /=63. Index 6='G', 16='Q', 36='k', 0='A' → "GQkA". But the standard PNG base64 is "iVBORw0KGgoAAAAN..." — that's 8950 4E47 0D0A 1A09 0000 000D:
- 89 50 4E: 10001001 01010000 01001110 → 100010(34='i') 010101(21='V') 000001(1='B') 001110(14='O') → "iVBO" ✓
- 47 0D 0A: 01000111 00001101 00001010 → 010001(17='R') 110000(48='w') 110100(52='0') 001010(10='K') → "Rw0K" ✓
- 1A 09 00: 00011010 00001001 00000000 → 000110(6='G') 100000(32='g') 100100(36='k') 000000(0='A') → "GgkA" ✓ (I miscalculated the split earlier)
- 00 00 0D: → "AAAN" ✓
So "iVBORw0KGgkAAAAN" — hmm, but the famous prefix is "iVBORw0KGgoAAAAN". The difference is: the famous one is 8950 4E47 0D0A 1A0A?? No — the famous one is: iVBORw0KGgo = bytes 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 09 00? Let's try decoding "KGgo": K=10, G=6, g=32, o=40 → 001010 000110 100000 101000 → 00101000(0x28='(') 01101000('h') 00101000('(') — "Ggo" straddles bytes 1A 09 00? Hmm, I keep messing up the boundaries. Whatever — base64 forensics is a waste of time; binascii has already proven validity.

#### Thinking

Found it! .NET's signature: `8950 4e47 0d0a 1a0a` — the last byte is **0x0A**, but mine is **0x09**!

PNG signature: 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A **0A** — the final byte is LF (0x0A), not 09! I wrote 0x09 (that's the end of the IEND CRC, and I got confused). The actual PNG signature is: \x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n → 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A.

My file has 1A 09 — invalid signature. That's why Chrome and GDI rejected it, while my own validator (which used the same wrong constant) accepted it.

Fix: change the signature in make-sample-pngs.js to 0x0a and regenerate. Also, the images already uploaded to local R2 are broken files — regenerate and re-seed. The smoke test data in local dev is fine (D1), but the R2 objects contain bad PNGs. Easiest way: after regenerating the samples, re-upload via a new chapter? Or just re-run the entire local environment: local D1/R2 are disposable — but the easiest is to re-upload pages 1-6 to the existing chapter (they'll be appended as idx 6-11) — no wait, cleaner: wipe the local state. wrangler dev local storage is in .wrangler/state. Can I delete .wrangler/state/v3? That's the local simulation data — safe to reset (it's my own test data). Then re-run the migrations locally and re-run the smoke test.

Actually, even simpler: leave the old data and re-run the smoke test with a new user/comic (comic 2) using the fixed files. This is my own scratch data — fine to delete.

#### Thinking

No console messages. The error text was captured by the catch and displayed on the stage. "Cannot set properties of null (setting 'innerHTML')" — something in the try block set innerHTML on a null element.

Looking at the code flow of reader.html:
1. `await initHeader()` — initHeader does `document.getElementById("topbar")` — reader.html doesn't have an element with id="topbar"! The reader has a header with class reader-topbar but no id="topbar". initHeader runs `el.innerHTML = ...` → el is null → error!

Fix: add id="topbar" to the reader's header element. But initHeader writes a standard topbar into it — the reader's header has a different structure (back button, etc.). Options:
- Don't call initHeader in the reader. But we need ME for the watermark and login checks. Replace with a lightweight call: `ME = (await req('/api/me')).id ? ... `
- Or add a hidden `<div id="topbar" style="display:none">`. Hacky but works — though the reader's topbar doesn't have login/logout links. The watermark needs ME. Simplest and correct approach: call a small initMe() in the reader instead of initHeader().

Let's edit reader.html: replace `await initHeader();` with:
```js
const me = await req("/api/me"); ME = me.id ? me : null;
```
And add a link to the account? Not needed.

Also check dashboard.html — it has id="topbar" ✓. comic.html ✓, index ✓, login ✓.

找到原因：阅读器没有 `id="topbar"` 元素，`initHeader()` 写空对象。改成轻量获取登录态。

#### Thinking

The danmaku isn't showing up in the screenshot. Possible causes:
1. The send failed (check the network/console).
2. The danmaku spawned but flew away too quickly (screenshot delay is about 1-2 seconds, animation is 7-11 seconds — it should still be visible).
3. spawnDm places it at `top: random lane + 56px` — the viewport height is 448, innerHeight/5 = max(2, 89-1)=88 → lane is 0..87 * 36?? Wait: `Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(2, Math.floor(innerHeight / 5) - 1)) * 36 + 56` — innerHeight=448 → floor(448/5)-1 = 89 → max(2,89)=89 → random*89 → up to 88*36+56 = 3224px — way off-screen! The lane calculation is wrong: it should be a small number of lanes (e.g., innerHeight/40 ≈ 11 lanes), but I ended up using innerHeight/5 as the *lane count*. top = up to 3224px → invisible.

Fix: laneCount = Math.max(3, Math.floor((innerHeight - 130) / 40)); top = lane * 40 + 60.

Also, check whether the send actually succeeded: verify via the API that the danmaku was saved. Fix the CSS spawn position and retest.

如果你暂时不方便开通，备选方案是改用 D1 存图片（单页 ≤2MB 限制，代码需小改），但不推荐——R2 是正确架构。

{
  "content": "/**\n * 漫画平台 Worker API\n * 路由前缀 /api/*，其余路径由静态资源(assets)处理。\n */\n\nconst PREVIEW_PAGES = 3; // 付费漫画每章可免费试读的页数\nconst SESSION_DAYS = 30;\nconst MAX_DANMAKU_LEN = 100;\nconst ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = new Set([\n  \"image/jpeg\", \"image/png\", \"image/webp\", \"image/gif\", \"image/avif\",\n]);\n\n// ---------- 小工具 ----------\n\nfunction json(data, status = 200, headers = {}) {\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\n    status,\n    headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json; charset=utf-8\", ...headers },\n  });\n}\n\nconst err = (msg, status = 400) => json({ error: msg }, status);\n\nfunction randomHex(bytes) {\n  const a = new Uint8Array(bytes);\n  crypto.getRandomValues(a);\n  return [...a].map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n}\n\nasync function hashPassword(password, saltHex) {\n  const enc = new TextEncoder();\n  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(\"raw\", enc.encode(password), \"PBKDF2\", false, [\"deriveBits\"]);\n  const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);\n  const bits = await crypto.subtle.deriveBits(\n    { name: \"PBKDF2\", salt, iterations: 150_000, hash: \"SHA-256\" },\n    key, 256\n  );\n  return bufToHex(bits);\n}\n\nconst hexToBuf = (h) => Uint8Array.from(h.match(/../g).map((x) => parseInt(x, 16)));\nconst bufToHex = (b) => [...new Uint8Array(b)].map((x) => x.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\")).join(\"\");\n\nfunction sessionCookie(token, maxAgeDays = SESSION_DAYS) {\n  const maxAge = maxAgeDays > 0 ? maxAgeDays * 86400 : 0;\n  return `sid=${token}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/; Max-Age=${maxAge}`;\n}\n\nasync function parseBody(request) {\n  try { return await request.json(); } catch { return null; }\n}\n\n// ---------- 会话 / 鉴权 ----------\n\nasync function currentUser(request, env) {\n  const cookie = request.headers.get(\"cookie\") || \"\";\n  const m = cookie.match(/(?:^|;\\s*)sid=([0-9a-f]{64})/);\n  if (!m) return null;\n  const row = await env.DB.prepare(\n    `SELECT u.id, u.username, s.token, s.expires_at FROM sessions s\n     JOIN users u ON u.id = s.user_id WHERE s.token = ?`\n  ).bind(m[1]).first();\n  if (!row) return null;\n  if (row.expires_at < Date.now()) {\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token = ?\").bind(m[1]).run();\n    return null;\n  }\n  return row;\n}\n\nasync function createSession(env, userId) {\n  const token = randomHex(32);\n  await env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT INTO sessions (token, user_id, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n    .bind(token, userId, Date.now() + SESSION_DAYS * 86400_000).run();\n  return token;\n}\n\n// ---------- 权限检查 ----------\n\nasync function getComicWithAccess(env, comicId, user) {\n  const comic = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId).first();\n  if (!comic) return null;\n  let unlocked = comic.price_cents <= 0;\n  if (!unlocked && user && user.id === comic.owner_id) unlocked = true;\n  if (!unlocked && user) {\n    const u = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT 1 FROM unlocks WHERE user_id = ? AND comic_id = ?\")\n      .bind(user.id, comicId).first();\n    unlocked = !!u;\n  }\n  const isOwner = !!(user && user.id === comic.owner_id);\n  return { comic, unlocked, isOwner };\n}\n\nasync function requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, comicId) {\n  if (!user) return { e: err(\"需要登录\", 401) };\n  const comic = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId).first();\n  if (!comic) return { e: err(\"漫画不存在\", 404) };\n  if (comic.owner_id !== user.id) return { e: err(\"只有作者可以操作自己的漫画\", 403) };\n  return { comic };\n}\n\nasync function requireOwnerOfChapter(env, user, chapterId) {\n  if (!user) return { e: err(\"需要登录\", 401) };\n  const ch = await env.DB.prepare(\n    \"SELECT ch.*, c.owner_id FROM chapters ch JOIN comics c ON c.id = ch.comic_id WHERE ch.id = ?\"\n  ).bind(chapterId).first();\n  if (!ch) return { e: err(\"章节不存在\", 404) };\n  if (ch.owner_id !== user.id) return { e: err(\"只有作者可以操作\", 403) };\n  return { chapter: ch };\n}\n\n// ---------- 上传处理 ----------\n\nasync function readImages(form, env, field = \"files\") {\n  const entries = form.getAll(field).filter((f) => f instanceof File && f.size > 0);\n  if (entries.length === 0) return { e: err(\"没有收到文件\") };\n  if (entries.length > 100) return { e: err(\"单次最多上传 100 张\") };\n  const maxBytes = Number(env.MAX_UPLOAD_MB || 10) * 1024 * 1024;\n  for (const f of entries) {\n    if (!ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES.has(f.type)) return { e: err(`不支持的文件类型: ${f.type || f.name}`) };\n    if (f.size > maxBytes) return { e: err(`文件 ${f.name} 超过 ${env.MAX_UPLOAD_MB || 10}MB 限制`) };\n  }\n  return { files: entries };\n}\n\nasync function putImage(env, key, file) {\n  await env.BUCKET.put(key, file.stream(), {\n    httpMetadata: { contentType: file.type, cacheControl: \"private, no-store\" },\n  });\n  return key;\n}\n\n// ---------- API 路由 ----------\n\nconst routes = [\n  // ===== 账号 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/register$/, async (c, env) => {\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req);\n    if (!body) return err(\"需要 JSON body\");\n    const username = String(body.username || \"\").trim();\n    const password = String(body.password || \"\");\n    if (!/^[\\w\\p{Script=Han}.\\-]{2,30}$/u.test(username)) return err(\"用户名需 2-30 个字符（文字/数字/_-.）\");\n    if (password.length < 6) return err(\"密码至少 6 位\");\n    const exists = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT 1 FROM users WHERE username = ?\").bind(username).first();\n    if (exists) return err(\"用户名已被占用\", 409);\n    const salt = randomHex(16);\n    const hash = await hashPassword(password, salt);\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT INTO users (username, pass_hash, pass_salt) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(username, hash, salt).run();\n    const token = await createSession(env, meta.last_row_id);\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id, username }, 201, { \"set-cookie\": sessionCookie(token) });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/login$/, async (c, env) => {\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req);\n    if (!body) return err(\"需要 JSON body\");\n    const user = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = ?\")\n      .bind(String(body.username || \"\").trim()).first();\n    if (!user) return err(\"用户名或密码错误\", 401);\n    const hash = await hashPassword(String(body.password || \"\"), user.pass_salt);\n    if (hash !== user.pass_hash) return err(\"用户名或密码错误\", 401);\n    const token = await createSession(env, user.id);\n    return json({ id: user.id, username: user.username }, 200, { \"set-cookie\": sessionCookie(token) });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/logout$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (user) await env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token = ?\").bind(user.token).run();\n    return json({ ok: true }, 200, { \"set-cookie\": sessionCookie(\"\", 0) });\n  }],\n\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/me$/, async (c, env, user) =>\n    json(user ? { id: user.id, username: user.username } : { id: null })],\n\n  // ===== 漫画浏览 =====\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/comics$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    const mine = new URL(c.req.url).searchParams.get(\"mine\");\n    const sql = `\n      SELECT c.*, u.username AS owner_name,\n        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM chapters ch WHERE ch.comic_id = c.id) AS chapter_count,\n        COALESCE(c.cover_key,\n          (SELECT p.r2_key FROM pages p JOIN chapters ch ON ch.id = p.chapter_id\n           WHERE ch.comic_id = c.id ORDER BY ch.num, p.idx LIMIT 1)) AS cover\n      FROM comics c JOIN users u ON u.id = c.owner_id\n      ${mine && user ? \"WHERE c.owner_id = ?\" : \"WHERE c.published = 1\"}\n      ORDER BY c.created_at DESC, c.id DESC`;\n    const stmt = env.DB.prepare(sql);\n    const { results } = await (mine && user ? stmt.bind(user.id) : stmt).all();\n    return json({ comics: results });\n  }],\n\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const acc = await getComicWithAccess(env, Number(m[1]), user);\n    if (!acc) return err(\"漫画不存在\", 404);\n    if (!acc.comic.published && !acc.isOwner) return err(\"漫画不存在\", 404);\n    const { results: chapters } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"SELECT id, num, title, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pages p WHERE p.chapter_id = ch.id) AS page_count FROM chapters ch WHERE comic_id = ? ORDER BY num\")\n      .bind(acc.comic.id).all();\n    return json({\n      comic: acc.comic, owner: null, chapters,\n      unlocked: acc.unlocked, isOwner: acc.isOwner, previewPages: PREVIEW_PAGES,\n    });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 作者：漫画 CRUD =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/comics$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const title = String(body.title || \"\").trim();\n    if (!title || title.length > 80) return err(\"标题需 1-80 字符\");\n    const price = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(body.price_cents) || 0));\n    const published = body.published === false ? 0 : 1;\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO comics (owner_id, title, description, price_cents, published) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(user.id, title, String(body.description || \"\").slice(0, 2000), price, published).run();\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"PUT\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const title = body.title !== undefined ? String(body.title).trim() : r.comic.title;\n    if (!title || title.length > 80) return err(\"标题需 1-80 字符\");\n    const desc = body.description !== undefined ? String(body.description).slice(0, 2000) : r.comic.description;\n    const price = body.price_cents !== undefined ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(body.price_cents) || 0)) : r.comic.price_cents;\n    const published = body.published !== undefined ? (body.published ? 1 : 0) : r.comic.published;\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE comics SET title=?, description=?, price_cents=?, published=? WHERE id=?\")\n      .bind(title, desc, price, published, r.comic.id).run();\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  [\"DELETE\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    await deleteComicCascade(env, r.comic.id);\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)\\/cover$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    const form = await c.req.formData();\n    const files = form.getAll(\"file\").filter((f) => f instanceof File && f.size > 0);\n    if (files.length !== 1) return err(\"需要 1 个封面文件\");\n    const one = await readImages({ getAll: () => files }, env);\n    if (one.e) return one.e;\n    if (r.comic.cover_key) await env.BUCKET.delete(r.comic.cover_key);\n    const key = `c${r.comic.id}/cover/${Date.now()}-${randomHex(4)}`;\n    await putImage(env, key, one.files[0]);\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE comics SET cover_key=? WHERE id=?\").bind(key, r.comic.id).run();\n    return json({ cover_key: key });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 作者：章节 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/comics\\/(\\d+)\\/chapters$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfComic(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO chapters (comic_id, num, title) VALUES (?, (SELECT COALESCE(MAX(num),0)+1 FROM chapters WHERE comic_id = ?), ?)\")\n      .bind(r.comic.id, r.comic.id, String(body.title || \"\").slice(0, 80)).run();\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"DELETE\", /^\\/api\\/chapters\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfChapter(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    await deleteChapterCascade(env, r.chapter.id);\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 作者：页面上传 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/chapters\\/(\\d+)\\/pages$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const r = await requireOwnerOfChapter(env, user, Number(m[1]));\n    if (r.e) return r.e;\n    let form;\n    try { form = await c.req.formData(); } catch { return err(\"multipart 解析失败\"); }\n    const files = await readImages(form, env);\n    if (files.e) return files.e;\n    const max = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT COALESCE(MAX(idx), -1) AS mx FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ?\")\n      .bind(r.chapter.id).first();\n    let idx = max.mx + 1;\n    const added = [];\n    for (const f of files.files) {\n      const ext = (f.name.split(\".\").pop() || \"img\").toLowerCase().slice(0, 8);\n      const key = `c${r.chapter.comic_id}/ch${r.chapter.id}/p${idx}-${Date.now()}-${randomHex(4)}.${ext}`;\n      await putImage(env, key, f);\n      await env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT INTO pages (chapter_id, idx, r2_key) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n        .bind(r.chapter.id, idx, key).run();\n      added.push({ idx, r2_key: key });\n      idx++;\n    }\n    return json({ added: added.length, pages: added }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"DELETE\", /^\\/api\\/pages\\/(\\d+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const page = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"SELECT p.*, c.owner_id FROM pages p JOIN chapters ch ON ch.id = p.chapter_id JOIN comics c ON c.id = ch.comic_id WHERE p.id = ?\")\n      .bind(Number(m[1])).first();\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    if (!page) return err(\"页面不存在\", 404);\n    if (page.owner_id !== user.id) return err(\"只有作者可以操作\", 403);\n    await env.BUCKET.delete(page.r2_key);\n    await env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE id = ?\").bind(page.id).run();\n    return json({ ok: true });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 阅读器 =====\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/chapters\\/(\\d+)\\/reader$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const ch = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"SELECT ch.*, c.title AS comic_title, c.price_cents, c.owner_id FROM chapters ch JOIN comics c ON c.id = ch.comic_id WHERE ch.id = ?\")\n      .bind(Number(m[1])).first();\n    if (!ch) return err(\"章节不存在\", 404);\n    const acc = await getComicWithAccess(env, ch.comic_id, user);\n    const { results: pages } = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT id, idx, r2_key FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ? ORDER BY idx\").bind(ch.id).all();\n    const canSee = (p) => acc.unlocked || acc.isOwner || p.idx < PREVIEW_PAGES;\n    return json({\n      chapter: { id: ch.id, num: ch.num, title: ch.title, comic_id: ch.comic_id, comic_title: ch.comic_title },\n      unlocked: acc.unlocked, isOwner: acc.isOwner, price_cents: ch.price_cents,\n      pages: pages.map((p) => ({\n        id: p.id, idx: p.idx,\n        url: canSee(p) ? `/api/img/${p.r2_key}` : null,\n        locked: !canSee(p),\n      })),\n    });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 图片（鉴权 + 防缓存）=====\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/img\\/(.+)$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    const key = decodeURIComponent(m[1]);\n    const page = await env.DB.prepare(\n      `SELECT p.idx, ch.id AS chapter_id, c.id AS comic_id, c.owner_id, c.price_cents\n       FROM pages p JOIN chapters ch ON ch.id = p.chapter_id JOIN comics c ON c.id = ch.comic_id\n       WHERE p.r2_key = ?`).bind(key).first();\n    if (page) {\n      const acc = await getComicWithAccess(env, page.comic_id, user);\n      if (!(acc.unlocked || acc.isOwner || page.idx < PREVIEW_PAGES)) return err(\"需要购买后查看\", 403);\n    } else {\n      const cover = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT id FROM comics WHERE cover_key = ?\").bind(key).first();\n      if (!cover) return err(\"图片不存在\", 404); // 封面公开可看\n    }\n    const obj = await env.BUCKET.get(key);\n    if (!obj) return err(\"图片不存在\", 404);\n    return new Response(obj.body, {\n      headers: {\n        \"content-type\": obj.httpMetadata?.contentType || \"application/octet-stream\",\n        \"cache-control\": \"private, no-store\",\n        \"x-content-type-options\": \"nosniff\",\n      },\n    });\n  }],\n\n  // ===== 弹幕 =====\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/danmaku$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    const u = new URL(c.req.url).searchParams;\n    const chId = Number(u.get(\"chapterId\"));\n    const pageIdx = Number(u.get(\"pageIdx\"));\n    if (!chId || Number.isNaN(pageIdx)) return err(\"需要 chapterId 和 pageIdx\");\n    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      `SELECT d.id, d.t_ms, d.text, w.username FROM danmaku d\n       LEFT JOIN users w ON w.id = d.user_id\n       WHERE d.chapter_id = ? AND d.page_idx = ? ORDER BY d.t_ms LIMIT 500`)\n      .bind(chId, pageIdx).all();\n    return json({ danmaku: results });\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/danmaku$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录后发送弹幕\", 401);\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const text = String(body.text || \"\").trim().slice(0, MAX_DANMAKU_LEN);\n    if (!text) return err(\"弹幕内容不能为空\");\n    const chId = Number(body.chapterId);\n    const pageIdx = Number(body.pageIdx);\n    const tMs = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(body.tMs) || 0));\n    const ch = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT 1 FROM chapters WHERE id = ?\").bind(chId).first();\n    if (!ch) return err(\"章节不存在\", 404);\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO danmaku (chapter_id, page_idx, t_ms, user_id, text) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(chId, pageIdx, tMs, user.id, text).run();\n    return json({ id: meta.last_row_id, text }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  // ===== Mock 支付 =====\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/orders$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const body = await parseBody(c.req) || {};\n    const acc = await getComicWithAccess(env, Number(body.comicId), user);\n    if (!acc) return err(\"漫画不存在\", 404);\n    if (acc.comic.price_cents <= 0) return err(\"该漫画免费\");\n    if (acc.unlocked) return err(\"已购买\");\n    const { meta } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      \"INSERT INTO orders (user_id, comic_id, amount_cents) VALUES (?, ?, ?)\")\n      .bind(user.id, acc.comic.id, acc.comic.price_cents).run();\n    return json({ orderId: meta.last_row_id, amount_cents: acc.comic.price_cents, comicTitle: acc.comic.title }, 201);\n  }],\n\n  [\"POST\", /^\\/api\\/orders\\/(\\d+)\\/pay$/, async (c, env, user, m) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const order = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT * FROM orders WHERE id = ? AND user_id = ?\")\n      .bind(Number(m[1]), user.id).first();\n    if (!order) return err(\"订单不存在\", 404);\n    if (order.status === \"paid\") return json({ ok: true, alreadyPaid: true });\n    // Mock：直接标记支付成功并解锁（生产环境应替换为支付宝异步通知验签后解锁）\n    await env.DB.batch([\n      env.DB.prepare(\"UPDATE orders SET status='paid', paid_at=datetime('now') WHERE id=?\").bind(order.id),\n      env.DB.prepare(\"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO unlocks (user_id, comic_id) VALUES (?, ?)\").bind(user.id, order.comic_id),\n    ]);\n    return json({ ok: true, unlocked: true });\n  }],\n\n  [\"GET\", /^\\/api\\/orders$/, async (c, env, user) => {\n    if (!user) return err(\"需要登录\", 401);\n    const { results } = await env.DB.prepare(\n      `SELECT o.id, o.amount_cents, o.status, o.created_at, c.title AS comic_title\n       FROM orders o JOIN comics c ON c.id = o.comic_id WHERE o.user_id = ? ORDER BY o.id DESC LIMIT 100`)\n      .bind(user.id).all();\n    return json({ orders: results });\n  }],\n];\n\n// ---------- 级联删除 ----------\n\nasync function deleteChapterCascade(env, chapterId) {\n  const { results: pages } = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT r2_key FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ?\").bind(chapterId).all();\n  for (const p of pages) await env.BUCKET.delete(p.r2_key);\n  await env.DB.batch([\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM pages WHERE chapter_id = ?\").bind(chapterId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM danmaku WHERE chapter_id = ?\").bind(chapterId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM chapters WHERE id = ?\").bind(chapterId),\n  ]);\n}\n\nasync function deleteComicCascade(env, comicId) {\n  const { results: chs } = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT id FROM chapters WHERE comic_id = ?\").bind(comicId).all();\n  for (const ch of chs) await deleteChapterCascade(env, ch.id);\n  const comic = await env.DB.prepare(\"SELECT cover_key FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId).first();\n  if (comic?.cover_key) await env.BUCKET.delete(comic.cover_key);\n  await env.DB.batch([\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM orders WHERE comic_id = ?\").bind(comicId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM unlocks WHERE comic_id = ?\").bind(comicId),\n    env.DB.prepare(\"DELETE FROM comics WHERE id = ?\").bind(comicId),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// ---------- fetch 入口 ----------\n\nexport default {\n  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {\n    const url = new URL(request.url);\n    const path = url.pathname;\n    if (!path.startsWith(\"/api/\")) {\n      // 交给静态资源（正常情况下 assets 层已处理，这里兜底 404）\n      return env.ASSETS.fetch(request);\n    }\n    const user = await currentUser(request, env);\n    for (const [method, pattern, handler] of routes) {\n      if (request.method !== method) continue;\n      const m = path.match(pattern);\n      if (!m) continue;\n      try {\n        return await handler(request, env, user, m);\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error(`[api] ${method} ${path}`, e);\n        return err(\"服务器内部错误\", 500);\n      }\n    }\n    return err(\"接口不存在\", 404);\n  },\n};\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\user\\Documents\\Qoder\\2026-10-31\\59dfa34d\\src\\worker.js"

{
  "content": "/* 共享工具：API、顶栏、消息提示、购买弹窗 */\n\nasync function req(path, opts = {}) {\n  const r = await fetch(path, { credentials: \"same-origin\", ...opts });\n  const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({}));\n  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(data.error || `请求失败 (${r.status})`);\n  return data;\n}\nconst apiPost = (p, b) => req(p, { method: \"POST\", headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify(b ?? {}) });\nconst apiPut = (p, b) => req(p, { method: \"PUT\", headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/json\" }, body: JSON.stringify(b ?? {}) });\nconst apiDel = (p) => req(p, { method: \"DELETE\" });\n\nfunction esc(s) {\n  return String(s ?? \"\").replace(/[&<>\"']/g, (ch) =>\n    ({ \"&\": \"&amp;\", \"<\": \"&lt;\", \">\": \"&gt;\", '\"': \"&quot;\", \"'\": \"&#39;\" }[ch]));\n}\n\nconst fmtPrice = (cents) => cents > 0 ? `¥${(cents / 100).toFixed(2)}` : \"免费\";\n\nlet ME = null; // {id, username} | null\n\nasync function initHeader(active) {\n  ME = await req(\"/api/me\");\n  if (!ME.id) ME = null;\n  const el = document.getElementById(\"topbar\");\n  el.innerHTML = `\n    <a class=\"logo\" href=\"/\">漫<b>域</b> MangaZone</a>\n    <nav>\n      <a href=\"/\" class=\"${active === \"store\" ? \"active\" : \"\"}\">书城</a>\n      <a href=\"/dashboard\" class=\"${active === \"dashboard\" ? \"active\" : \"\"}\">创作后台</a>\n    </nav>\n    <div class=\"spacer\"></div>\n    ${ME\n      ? `<span class=\"who\">${esc(ME.username)}</span><button class=\"btn small\" id=\"btn-logout\">退出</button>`\n      : `<a class=\"btn small\" href=\"/login\">登录 / 注册</a>`}`;\n  const btn = document.getElementById(\"btn-logout\");\n  if (btn) btn.onclick = async () => { await req(\"/api/logout\", { method: \"POST\" }); location.href = \"/\"; };\n}\n\nlet toastTimer = null;\nfunction toast(text, ms = 2500) {\n  let el = document.getElementById(\"toast\");\n  if (!el) {\n    el = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    el.id = \"toast\";\n    document.body.appendChild(el);\n  }\n  el.textContent = text;\n  el.style.display = \"block\";\n  clearTimeout(toastTimer);\n  toastTimer = setTimeout(() => { el.style.display = \"none\"; }, ms);\n}\n\n/**\n * 弹出 mock 支付窗口，购买指定漫画。成功后返回 true。\n * 生产环境：此处应替换为支付宝收银台（下单 -> 跳转/唤起 -> 异步通知验签 -> 解锁）。\n */\nasync function purchaseDialog(comicId, comicTitle, amountCents) {\n  const order = await apiPost(\"/api/orders\", { comicId });\n  return await new Promise((resolve) => {\n    const bg = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    bg.className = \"modal-bg\";\n    bg.innerHTML = `\n      <div class=\"modal\">\n        <h3>确认支付（模拟）</h3>\n        <p class=\"muted\">${esc(comicTitle)}</p>\n        <p style=\"font-size:26px;color:var(--warn);margin:6px 0\">${fmtPrice(order.amount_cents)}</p>\n        <p class=\"muted\" style=\"font-size:12px\">原型演示：本按钮模拟支付宝收款成功，未接入真实支付。</p>\n        <div class=\"row\" style=\"margin-top:16px\">\n          <button class=\"btn primary grow\" id=\"do-pay\">模拟支付成功</button>\n          <button class=\"btn grow\" id=\"do-cancel\">取消</button>\n        </div>\n      </div>`;\n    document.body.appendChild(bg);\n    const close = (v) => { bg.remove(); resolve(v); };\n    bg.querySelector(\"#do-cancel\").onclick = () => close(false);\n    bg.querySelector(\"#do-pay\").onclick = async () => {\n      const btn = bg.querySelector(\"#do-pay\");\n      btn.disabled = true; btn.textContent = \"支付中…\";\n      try {\n        await apiPost(`/api/orders/${order.orderId}/pay`, {});\n        toast(\"支付成功，已解锁全部章节 🎉\");\n        close(true);\n      } catch (e) { toast(e.message); close(false); }\n    };\n  });\n}\n\n/** 未登录则跳登录页，登录后回到当前页 */\nfunction needLogin() {\n  if (ME && ME.id) return true;\n  location.href = \"/login?next=\" + encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search);\n  return false;\n}\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\user\\Documents\\Qoder\\2026-10-31\\59dfa34d\\public\\js\\common.js"
}

}

   }\n        const d = document.createElement(\"div\");\n        d.className = \"lock-panel\";\n        d.innerHTML = `\n          <div class=\"lock-icon\">🔒</div>\n          <p>本章第 ${p.idx + 1} const imgs = [...stage.querySelectorAll(\"img.page-img\")];\n          const mid = innerHeight / 2;\n          let best = 0;\n          imgs.forEach((im, i) => { const r = im.getBoundingClientRect(); if (r.top <= mid && r.bottom > 40) best = Number(im.dataset.arrIdx); });\n          if (best !== cur) { cur = best; updateCounter(); loadPageDanmaku(); }\n        });\n      }, { passive: true });\n\n      // ---------- 弹幕引擎 ----------\n      async function loadPageDanmaku() {\n        curPageIdx = data.pages[cur]?.idx ?? 0;\n        pageStart.set(curPageIdx, performance.now());\n        if (!dmCache.has(curPageIdx)) {\n          try {\n            const d = await req(`/api/danmaku?chapterId=${chId}&pageIdx=${curPageIdx}`);\n            dmCache.set(curPageIdx, d.danmaku);\n          } toast(e.message); }\n      }\n      document.getElementById(\"dm-send\").onclick = sendDm;\n      input.addEventListener(\"keydown\", (e) => { if (e.key === \"Enter\") sendDm(); });\n\n n < 256; n++) { c = n; for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1; t[n] = c; }\n    return t;\n  })());\n  c = -1;\n  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i++) c = table[(c ^ buf[i]) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);\n  return (c ^ -1) >>> 0;\n}\n\nfunction chunk(type, data) {\n  const len = Buffer.alloc(4); len.writeUInt32BE(data.length);\n  const td = Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(type), data]);\n  const crc = Buffer.alloc(4); crc.writeUInt32BE(crc32(td));\n  return Buffer.concat([len, td, crc]);\n}\n\nfunction makePng(w, h, painter) {\n  const raw = Buffer.alloc(h * (1 + w * 3));\n  let o = 0;\n  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {\n    raw[o++] = 0;\n    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {\n      const [r, g, b] = painter(x, y);\n      raw[o++] = r; raw[o++] = g; raw[o++] = b;\n    }\n  }\n  const ihdr = Buffer.alloc(13);\n  ihdr.writeUInt32BE(w, 0); ihdr.writeUInt32BE(h, 4);\n  ihdr[8] = 8; ihdr[9] = 2; // 8-bit RGB\n  return Buffer.concat([\n    Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x09]),\n    chunk(\"IHDR\", ihdr),\n    chunk(\"IDAT\", zlib.deflateSync(raw, { level: 6 })),\n    chunk(\"IEND\", Buffer.alloc(0)),\n  ]);\n}\n\n// 用 5x7 位图点阵把页码画成大色块图案（辨识度足够测试用）\nconst DIGITS = {\n  \"0\": [\"11111\", \"10001\", \"10011\", \"10101\", \"11001\", \"10001\", \"11111\"],\n  \"1\": [\"00100\", \"01100\", \"00100\", \"00100\", \"00100\", \"00100\", \"01110\"],\n  \"2\": [\"11111\", \"00001\", \"11111\", \"10000\", \"10000\", \"10000\", \"11111\"],\n  \"3\": [\"11111\", \"00001\", \"11111\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"11111\"],\n  \"4\": [\"10001\", \"10001\", \"11111\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"00001\"],\n  \"5\": [\"11111\", \"10000\", \"11111\", \"00001\", \"00001\", \"10001\", \"11111\"],\n};\n\nfunction painterFor(num, hue) {\n  const s = String(num);\n  const cell = 60, ox = 40, oy = 100;\n  const bg1 = hsl(hue, 30, 18), bg2 = hsl(hue + 40, 45, 38);\n  const fg = hsl(hue + 20, 85, 65);\n  return (x, y) => {\n    const t = y / 720;\n    const base = [\n      Math.round(bg1[0] + (bg2[0] - bg1[0]) * t),\n      Math.round(bg1[1] + (bg2[1] - bg1[1]) * t),\n      Math.round(bg1[2] + (bg2[2] - bg1[2]) * t),\n    ];\n    const di = Math.floor((x - ox) / cell);\n    const px = (x - ox) % cell, py = y - oy;\n    if (di >= 0 && di < s.length && py >= 0 && py < 7 * cell) {\n      const row = DIGITS[s[di]][Math.floor(py / cell)];\n      if (row[Math.floor(px / (cell / 5)) - 0] === undefined) return base;\n      if (row[Math.min(4, Math.floor(px / (cell / 5)))] === \"1\") return fg;\n    }\n    if (y > 640 && (Math.floor(x / 40) + Math.floor(y / 40)) % 2 === 0) return hsl(hue, 20, 26);\n    return base;\n  };\n}\n\nfunction hsl(h, s, l) {\n  s /= 100; l /= 100;\n  const k = (n) => (n + h / 30) % 12;\n  const a = s * Math.min(l, 1 - l);\n  const f = (n) => 255 * (l - a * Math.max(-1, Math.min(k(n) - 3, Math.min(9 - k(n), 1))));\n  return [Math.round(f(0)), Math.round(f(8)), Math.round(f(4))];\n}\n\nconst out = path.join(__dirname, \"..\", \"sample-assets\");\nfs.mkdirSync(out, { recursive: true });\nfor (let i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {\n  const png = makePng(600, 720, painterFor(i, i * 47));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(out, `page${i}.png`), png);\n  console.log(`page${i}.png ${(png.length / 1024).toFixed(1)}KB`);\n}\n",
  "file_path": "C:\\Users\\user\\Documents\\Qoder\\2026-10-31\\59dfa34d\\scripts\\make-sample-pngs.js"
}
